# This work vs FMM-Lille vs Perminov over C (non-commutative best ranks)

Auto-generated by `GenerateSourceComparison` from `docs/catalog.json` + `references/catalogs/fmm-lille-catalog.json` + `references/catalogs/perminov-catalog.json` — do not edit. Machine twin: `us-vs-fmm-vs-perminov-C.json`.

**Field-scoped to C**: a scheme appears only if valid over C. `us_derived` = best rank our own constructors/closure produced (the only column we claim as a discovery); `us_imported` = best rank among schemes we carry as imports. FMM-Lille carries no per-entry field tag and is treated as characteristic-0 (valid over Q/R/C only), so it is absent from the Z/F₂/F₃ tables.

`vs best` legend: **win** = our derived work beats both external catalogs; **tie_derived** = our derived work matches the external best; **tie_imported** = we hold the external best but only via an import (our derivation is worse or absent — at the frontier, just not re-derived, so NOT a loss); **loss_derived** = an external catalog beats everything we hold AND the winner is an FMM-*derived* composition (no cited reference) — a gap in our DERIVATION, re-derivable; **loss_import** = the winner is a *published* scheme (Perminov, or FMM with a cited reference) — a gap in our IMPORTS, we should carry it; **carried** = import-only coverage with no external comparator. The `FMM src` column marks whether the FMM-Lille entry is `pub` (cites a published result) or `deriv` (FMM's own composition).
